Node & Engine Requirements\\n\\n- **Node.js 14+** required in SVGO v3 (dropped Node 10 and 12 support).\\n\\n\\n## 2. CLI Changes\\n\\n- The command-line interface (CLI) uses the same commands but has **different config handling**.\\n- **.svgo.yml** is now supported.\\n\\n### Example Usage\\n\\n```sh\\n# Optimize a file\\nsvgo input.svg -o output.svg\\n\\n# Using a config file (now .yml is supported)\\nsvgo --config=path/to/svgo.config.js input.svg\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 3. Configuration Format Changes\\n\\n- Short plugin names (e.g., `\\\"removeDoctype\\\"`) must be fully specified (e.g., `\\\"name\\\": \\\"removeDoctype\\\"`).\\n- Configuration files now **must export an object**, not a function.\\n\\n**Before (v2):**\\n\\n```js\\nmodule.exports = {\\n  plugins: ['removeDoctype', 'removeXMLProcInst']\\n};\\n```\\n\\n**After (v3):**\\n\\n```js\\nmodule.exports = {\\n  plugins: [\\n    { name: 'removeDoctype' },\\n    { name: 'removeXMLProcInst' }\\n  ]\\n};\\n```\\n\\n**YAML Example:**\\n\\n```yaml\\nplugins:\\n  - name: removeDoctype\\n  - name: removeXMLProcInst\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n## 4. Here’s what you need to know and how to fix common issues:\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **Common Causes and Solutions**\\n\\n### 1. **SVGO v3 API is Asynchronous**\\n\\nIn SVGO v3, the API changed from synchronous to asynchronous. So, functions like `optimize()` now return a **Promise** rather than a direct result.\\n\\n**Old usage (v2):**\\n```js\\nconst result = svgo.optimize(svgString);\\nconsole.log(result.data);\\n```\\n\\n**New usage (v3):**\\n```js\\nconst result = await svgo.optimize(svgString);\\nconsole.log(result.data);\\n```\\nOr, using `.then()`:\\n```js\\nsvgo.optimize(svgString).then(result => {\\n  console.log(result.data);\\n});\\n```\\n\\nIf you forget to `await`, you’ll get a **Promise**, not the result object, leading to type errors.\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 2. **TypeScript Return Type Updates**\\n\\nSVGO v3 now returns a `Promise<OptimizedSvg>` from `svgo.optimize`, not just `OptimizedSvg`.  \\nIf you have TypeScript type errors, update your types:\\n\\n**Old:**\\n```ts\\nfunction optimizeSvg(svg: string): OptimizedSvg {\\n  return svgo.optimize(svg);\\n}\\n```\\n**New:**\\n```ts\\nasync function optimizeSvg(svg: string): Promise<OptimizedSvg> {\\n  return await svgo.optimize(svg);\\n}\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 3. **Correct Import**\\n\\nMake sure you’re importing SVGO correctly:\\n```js\\nimport { optimize } from 'svgo';\\n// or, for CommonJS\\n// const { optimize } = require('svgo');\\n```\\n\\n---\\n\\n### 4. **Error Example**\\n\\n**Error:**\\n> Type 'Promise<OptimizedSvg>' is not assignable to type 'OptimizedSvg'.\\n\\n**Solution:**  \\nChange the function or variable to correctly handle the returned Promise.\\n\\n---\\n\\n## **References**\\n\\n- [SVGO v3 Migration Guide](https://github.com/svg/svgo/blob/main/MIGRATION_GUIDE.md#v3-to-v2)\\n- [SVGO v3 API Docs](https://github.com/svg/svgo#nodejs)\\n\\nIf you post your exact error message and code, I can help you fix it line-by-line!\",\"annotations\":null},\"bing_searches\":null,\"annotations\":null}","detailedContent":"{\"type\":\"output_text\",\"text\":{\"value\":\"When using **SVGO v3**, if you encounter a “return type error,” it’s likely related to TypeScript, updated API signatures, or improper async handling. Old: `'cleanupIDs'`\\n  - New: `'cleanupIds'`\\n- The `cleanupIds` plugin **no longer accepts the `prefix` parameter**.\\n  - If you need to prefix IDs, use the new `prefixIds` plugin (order matters: put `prefixIds` after `cleanupIds`):\\n\\n    ```js\\n    export default {\\n      plugins: [\\n        { name: 'cleanupIds' },\\n        { name: 'prefixIds', params: { prefix: 'your-prefix' } }\\n      ],\\n    }\\n    ```\\n\\n### Breaking Plugin Loader Changes\\n- Plugins no longer use the `active` property.\\n  - A named plugin is enabled, omitted means disabled.\\n  - Example:\\n    ```js\\n    // Old v2:\\n    { name: 'removeDoctype', active: true },\\n    { name: 'removeComments', active: false },\\n\\n    // New v3:\\n    { name: 'removeDoctype' }\\n    // Just remove the second plugin if not active\\n    ```\\n\\n- **`extendDefaultPlugins` helper removed**  \\n  Use the `overrides` property in `preset-default` to override built-in plugins:\\n\\n    ```js\\n    export default {\\n      plugins: [\\n        {\\n          name: \\\"preset-default\\\",\\n          params: {\\n            overrides: {\\n              collapseGroups: false,\\n            },\\n          },\\n        },\\n      ],\\n    }\\n    ```\\n\\n### Configuration Syntax\\n- List plugins by name or as config objects. Anything named is enabled.\\n- Example v3 config:\\n\\n    ```js\\n    export default {\\n      plugins: [\\n        {\\n          name: \\\"preset-default\\\",\\n          params: {\\n            overrides: {\\n              cleanupIds: false,             // disables\\n              sortAttrs: false,              // disables\\n            },\\n          },\\n        },\\n        { name: \\\"prefixIds\\\", params: { prefix: \\\"my-prefix\\\" } },\\n      ],\\n    }\\n    ```\\n\\n### Node Version Requirement\\n- SVGO v3 requires Node.js \\u003e= 14.0.0\\n\\n### Other Removals\\n- The JavaScript optimize API no longer returns the `info` property (width/height), or `error`/`modernError`.
